data-mw=\"{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;langx&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Langx&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ar&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ولاية href=\"./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text\" about=\"#mwt16\" id=\"mwEA\">) is an Algerian <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Provinces_of_Algeria\" title=\"Provinces of Algeria\" id=\"mwEQ\">province</a> <i id=\"mwEg\">(<a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wilayah\" title=\"Wilayah\" class=\"mw-redirect\" It is in the Algerian Sahara.</p>\n\n<meta property=\"mw:PageProp/toc\" id=\"mwFA\" data-mw=\"{&quot;autoGenerated&quot;:true}\"></section><section data-mw-section-id=\"1\" id=\"mwFQ\" aria-labelledby=\"Geography\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Geography\">Geography</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Touggourt_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1\" title=\"Edit section: Geography\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwFg\">Touggourt Province is located in the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algerian_Desert\" title=\"Algerian Desert\" id=\"mwFw\">Algerian Sahara</a> and has an area of 17,428<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwGA\">&nbsp;</span>km². Its main settlement and capital, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Touggourt\" title=\"Touggourt\" id=\"mwGQ\">Touggourt</a>, is located on an oasis.</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwGg\">It is delimited by:</p>\n\n<ul id=\"mwGw\"><li id=\"mwHA\">to the north by the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/El_M'Ghair_Province\" title=\"El M'Ghair Province\" id=\"mwHQ\">El M'Ghair Province</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ouled_Djellal_Province\" title=\"Ouled Djellal Province\" id=\"mwHg\">ouled Djellal Province</a>;</li>\n<li id=\"mwHw\">to the east by the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/El_Oued_Province\" title=\"El Oued Province\" id=\"mwIA\">El Oued Province</a>;</li>\n<li id=\"mwIQ\">to the west by the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Djelfa_Province\" title=\"Djelfa Province\" id=\"mwIg\">Djelfa Province</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ghardaia_Province\" title=\"Ghardaia Province\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwIw\">Ghardaia Province</a>;</li>\n<li id=\"mwJA\">and to the south by the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ouargla_Province\" title=\"Ouargla Province\" id=\"mwJQ\">Ouargla Province</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illizi_Province\" title=\"Illizi Province\" id=\"mwJg\">Illizi Province</a> .</li></ul>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"2\" id=\"mwJw\" aria-labelledby=\"History\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"History\">History</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Touggourt_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2\" title=\"Edit section: History\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwKA\">The wilaya of Touggourt was created on November 26, 2019.<sup about=\"#mwt19\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-2\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-2&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-2\" id=\"mwKQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwKg\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwKw\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwLA\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<p id=\"mwLQ\">Previously, it was a <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delegated_wilaya?action=edit&amp;redlink=1\" title=\"Delegated wilaya (page does not exist)\" class=\"new\" typeof=\"mw:LocalizedAttrs\" id=\"mwLg\" data-mw-i18n=\"{&quot;title&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;x-page&quot;,&quot;key&quot;:&quot;red-link-title&quot;,&quot;params&quot;:[&quot;Delegated wilaya&quot;]}}\">delegated wilaya</a>, created according to the law n° 15–140 of May 27, 2015, creating administrative districts in certain wilayas and fixing the specific rules related to them, as well as the list of municipalities that are attached to it. Before 2019, it was attached to the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ouargla_Province\" title=\"Ouargla Province\" id=\"mwLw\">Ouargla Province</a>.</p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"3\" id=\"mwMA\" aria-labelledby=\"Organization_of_the_wilaya\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Organization_of_the_wilaya\">Organization of the wilaya</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Touggourt_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3\" title=\"Edit section: Organization of the wilaya\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwMQ\">During the administrative breakdown of 2019, the delegated wilaya of Touggourt is made up of 6 <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Districts_of_Algeria\" title=\"Districts of Algeria\" id=\"mwMg\">districts</a> and 14 <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communes_of_Algeria\" title=\"Communes of Algeria\" id=\"mwMw\">communes</a>.</p>\n\n<section data-mw-section-id=\"4\" id=\"mwNA\" aria-labelledby=\"List_of_subdivisions\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"List_of_subdivisions\">List of subdivisions</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span
